[0m$ [0msed -n '23,27p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/changelog/v2.1.0.0.md; echo "--- lint"; python3 .skilled/skills/sk-doc/sk-create-changelog/scripts/check_changelog_shape.py .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/changelog/v2.1.0.0.md; echo "exit=$?"
- **Each extension is one ledger event.** The `run_extended` event records the extension, and the run re-synthesizes after the new iterations.
- **The prior synthesis is kept.** The synthesis or review report is copied to a versioned snapshot before the first new iteration. An existing snapshot is never overwritten.
- **Unsafe extensions are refused.** Three cases stop with a named error. They are a run that is not complete, a fan-out config, and a value that is not a positive integer.

# v1.11.18.0, A Review Run Holds Below Its Minimum Iterations

The minimum-iteration floor applied to research runs only. A review that reached a stop candidate before its floor could stop early. The floor now applies to review runs too.

### `:auto` Setup Resolution

Setup contract: see `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/references/workflows/auto-mode-contract.md`.

Under `execution_mode = AUTONOMOUS` (from the `:auto` suffix), follow the three-tier flow:

1. **Tier 1 — Resolve confidently** (contract §1): parse `$ARGUMENTS` flags + `PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS:` block (§2) + the Default Resolution Table below (§3). When every required field is resolved, persist to `{artifact_dir}/deep-research-config.json` (shape: `researchTopic`, `specFolder`, `maxIterations`, `convergenceThreshold`, `antiConvergence.convergenceMode`, reserved `antiConvergence.divergent: {}`, `executionMode: "auto"`, `resource_map.emit`, `config.executor.*`), bind runtime YAML placeholders, set `STATUS: PASSED`, load `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-research.yaml` with `execution_mode` bound to `auto`. End §0.

2. **Tier 2 — Targeted ask** (contract §1): when 1-2 required fields are genuinely ambiguous AND no default exists, emit ONE narrow question per ambiguous field. Command-specific Tier-2-eligible fields (per the Default Resolution Table below): `spec_folder`. **Ordering rule**: none needed. Missing `research_topic` is absence, not ambiguity — go to Tier 3.

3. **Tier 3 — Fail fast** (contract §4): emit the named-missing-inputs error format with `/deep:research:auto` as the command name. Exit non-zero. Do not load YAML.

### `:auto` Setup Resolution

Setup contract: see `.skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/references/workflows/auto-mode-contract.md`.

Under `execution_mode = AUTONOMOUS` (from the `:auto` suffix), follow the three-tier flow:

1. **Tier 1 — Resolve confidently** (contract §1): parse `$ARGUMENTS` flags + `PRE-BOUND SETUP ANSWERS:` block (§2) + the Default Resolution Table below (§3). When every required field is resolved, resolve `{artifact_dir}` and persist to `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-config.json` (shape: `reviewTarget`, `reviewTargetType`, `reviewDimensions`, `reviewFocus`, `specFolder`, `maxIterations`, `convergenceThreshold`, `antiConvergence.convergenceMode`, reserved `antiConvergence.divergent: {}`, `stopPolicy`, `executionMode: "auto"`, `lineageMode`, `resource_map.emit`, `config.executor.*`), bind runtime YAML placeholders, set `STATUS: PASSED`, load `.skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml` with `execution_mode` bound to `auto`. End §0.

2. **Tier 2 — Targeted ask** (contract §1): when 1-2 required fields are genuinely ambiguous AND no default exists, emit ONE narrow question per ambiguous field. Command-specific Tier-2-eligible fields (per the Default Resolution Table below): `review_target_type`, `spec_folder`. **Ordering rule**: if `review_target_type` is ambiguous, ask only for `review_target_type` first — the answer may make `spec_folder` self-evident on the next Tier 1 pass. Missing `review_target` is absence, not ambiguity — go to Tier 3.

3. **Tier 3 — Fail fast** (contract §4): emit the named-missing-inputs error format with `/deep:review:auto` as the command name. Exit non-zero. Do not load YAML.

//     (e.g. a `.sqlite.pre-<op>.*.bak` snapshot), never a concrete file.
      //   - a `.doctor-<route>.` dotfile is a lock/pid/state/log the doctor
      //     orchestrator writes and tears down within a single run.
      // The skip is unconditional (before the existence check) so a clean checkout
      // and a machine that happens to hold a leftover artifact behave identically.
      if (text.charAt(m.index + m[0].length) === '*') continue;

      if (!token) continue;

      // A path under the gitignored runtime state root is written while a command runs, so a clean
      // checkout never holds it. The tracked README under it stays checked.
      if (/^\.skilled\/skills\/\.state\//.test(token) && path.basename(token) !== 'README.md') continue;

      // or a fresh worktree holds none of them.

      if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(rootDir, token))) {
        add(lineNo, 'skill-asset', token, 'path does not exist on disk');
      }

[0m$ [0msed -n '235,265p' README.md; echo "=== grep"; grep -cE -- "depth-mode|expansive|--extend|custom dimension|dimensions" README.md
### You Just Ask

You never need to remember a command. Describe the task in plain words and the session handles the rest. On every prompt, the [skill advisor](#skill-advisor) reads the request and tells the agent which skill fits. When a workflow fits too, the brief names its command and how to run it: start it now, start it with its setup questions or offer it to you first.

The session automation covers the rest of the path. The [spec gate](#spec-gate) asks once which spec folder the work belongs to. The [session lifecycle](#session-lifecycle) hooks prime the context at start and save continuity at stop and compaction. Near the context limit, [session handover](#session-handover) carries the work into a fresh session.

The commands below are what runs underneath. Type one yourself only when you want a specific workflow or mode.

### Spec Kit Commands

`/speckit:complete` runs the whole path: intake, research, plan, build, verify and save. `/speckit:plan` stops after the plan, so someone can review it before any code is written. `/speckit:implement` builds from an existing plan. Each one accepts `:auto` to run without stopping or `:confirm` to pause at each step.

`/speckit:save` and `/speckit:resume` carry the work across sessions, as [Continuity](#continuity) describes. `/speckit:search` runs the trigger index and the ripgrep recipes over spec docs and skill docs. `/speckit:handoff` hands the work to a fresh session now, instead of waiting for the context limit.

### Deep Research And Deep Review

The deep loops run unattended and stop only when their own evidence says the work is done. `/deep:research` investigates a question one focused pass at a time. Results grow in one research document inside the spec folder. `/deep:review` audits code in passes, ranks every finding P0, P1 or P2 and never edits the code it reviews.

Each pass runs in a fresh context against state kept on disk. A script, not the agent, decides when to stop. A review stays open while a P0 is active or a required check still fails, and it ends in a PASS, CONDITIONAL or FAIL verdict. A loop survives crashes and new sessions, because its progress lives in files.

Two opt-in flags let a loop reach further. `--depth-mode=expansive` keeps a frontier of sub-questions or sub-targets and turns a stop into another pass while one stays open, though never past the iteration cap. `--extend=N` adds N passes to a finished run in the same session. A review can also track custom dimensions beside its four defaults.

A loop can also spread across several AI CLIs at once. Each executor runs its own lineage, and for a review the strictest verdict wins the merge. A pool caps how many lineages run at once. A stall watchdog stops a lineage that stops making progress, and a cost cap bounds what each one spends. The [Deep Loop README](.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/README.md) describes both modes and the shared runtime.

### CLI Orchestration

Your main CLI does not have to handle every task itself. The CLI orchestration hub hands a task to another AI CLI on the same machine and reads the result back. The agent you are talking to stays in charge. It writes the brief, waits for the answer and checks it before using it.

This lets you mix subscriptions. You can work in one CLI and send research, reviews or bulk work to another, billed to that CLI's own plan. The bridges are provider agnostic. Each CLI runs whatever providers and models you have set up in it. That is why Skilled supports eight runtimes: whichever plans you already pay for, one runtime is your main CLI and the others receive the work you hand off.

You ask for it in plain words, for example "use cli-opencode to review this diff". The advisor routes the request to the matching bridge. The bridge checks that its CLI is installed and that the dispatch passes its hard rules, then runs it. The deep loops use the same bridges, so one research or review run can spread its passes across several models. [Runtimes And CLI Bridges](#10--runtimes-and-cli-bridges) lists all eight.

import os,subprocess,re
for mode in ['deep-research','deep-review']:
    print('==',mode)
    base=f'.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/{mode}/feature-catalog'
    # head dirs
    for d in sorted(os.listdir(base)):
        p=os.path.join(base,d)
        if os.path.isdir(p):
            n=len([f for f in os.listdir(p) if f.endswith('.md')])
            print(' head',d,n)
    txt=open(os.path.join(base,'feature-catalog.md')).read()
    print(' table rows:')
    for l in txt.split('\n'):
        m=re.match(r'\|\s*([A-Za-z /-]+?)\s*\|\s*(\d+) features?\s*\|',l)
        if m: print('  ',m.group(1),m.group(2))

import subprocess,os,re
base='21c4b325b5a'
for mode in ['deep-research','deep-review']:
    root=f'.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/{mode}/feature-catalog'
    out=subprocess.run(['git','-c','core.fsmonitor=false','ls-tree','-r','--name-only',base,'--',root],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout.split()
    from collections import Counter
    c=Counter()
    for p in out:
        parts=p.split('/')
        if parts[-1]=='feature-catalog.md': continue
        c[parts[-2]]+=1
    print('== base',mode,dict(c))
    txt=subprocess.run(['git','-c','core.fsmonitor=false','show',base+':'+root+'/feature-catalog.md'],capture_output=True,text=True).stdout
    for l in txt.split('\n'):
        m=re.match(r'\|\s*([A-Za-z /-]+?)\s*\|\s*(\d+) features?\s*\|',l)
        if m: print('  base table',m.group(1),m.group(2))

import re
for mode in ['deep-research','deep-review']:
    p=f'.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/{mode}/manual-testing-playbook/manual-testing-playbook.md'
    lines=open(p).read().split('\n')
    sec=None; actual={}; claim={}
    for i,l in enumerate(lines,1):
        m=re.match(r'^## (\d+)\.',l)
        if m: sec=int(m.group(1)); actual.setdefault(sec,0)
        if re.match(r'^### (?:DR|DRV|CP|RV|DR-R)\d*',l) or re.match(r'^### DR-R\d+',l):
            actual[sec]=actual.get(sec,0)+1
        c=re.search(r'This category covers (\d+) scenario',l)
        if c: claim[sec]=(i,int(c.group(1)))
    print('==',mode)
    for s in sorted(actual):
        if s in claim:
            mark='OK' if claim[s][1]==actual[s] else 'MISMATCH'
            print(f' sec {s}: claim {claim[s][1]} actual {actual[s]} line {claim[s][0]} {mark}')
        elif actual[s]>0:
            print(f' sec {s}: no claim, actual {actual[s]}')

function buildStopDecisionGraphFailurePayload(error, args, loopType, files) {
  const input = buildStopDecisionInput(args, loopType, files, { failed: true });
  return {
    status: 'ok',
    data: null,
    graph_decision: null,
    graph_error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    stop_decision: buildStopDecision(input),
    loop_counters_json: {
      stuckCount: input.stuckCount,
      consecutiveFailureCount: input.consecutiveFailureCount,
    },
  };
}

async function main() {
  const args = parseArgs();
  assertKnownFlags(args, CONVERGENCE_FLAGS, inputError);
  const specFolder = validateNamespaceValue(ensureString(args, 'specFolder'), 'specFolder', inputError);
  const loopType = ensureString(args, 'loopType');
  const sessionId = validateNamespaceValue(ensureString(args, 'sessionId'), 'sessionId', inputError);
  if (loopType !== 'research' && loopType !== 'review' && loopType !== 'context') {
    throw inputError('loopType must be "research", "review", or "context"');
  }
  const stopDecisionRequested = args.stopDecision === true;
  if (stopDecisionRequested && loopType !== 'research' && loopType !== 'review') {
    throw inputError('--stop-decision is supported only for "research" and "review" loop types');
  }
  // Read the state files before the graph is opened so a bad request exits as
  // an input error instead of depending on graph availability.
  const stopDecisionFiles = stopDecisionRequested ? readStopDecisionFiles(args) : null;

  const ns = { specFolder, loopType, sessionId };
  const readOnly = args.readOnly === true;
  let db = null;

  try {
    db = await import('../lib/coverage-graph/coverage-graph-db.ts');
    if (readOnly) db.openReadOnlyDb();
    installSignalHandlers(() => db?.closeDb());
    maybeThrowTestFault();

    const signalsLib = await import('../lib/coverage-graph/coverage-graph-signals.ts');

[0m$ [0msed -n '1232,1380p' .skilled/commands/deep/assets/deep-review.yaml
step_check_convergence:
        action: "Branch on the stop decision that convergence.cjs computed in step_graph_convergence"
        algorithm: |
          The whole stop decision is computed by convergence.cjs from the review config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. This step performs no arithmetic and never recomputes or overrides the result.
          The ratio signals leave out thought iterations and every iteration whose status is error or timeout, and stuck_count is loop_counters_json.stuckCount.
          The script applies this fixed order, first match wins:
          1. if iteration_count >= max_iterations: STOP (maxIterationsReached). This terminal cap is evaluated before every veto, the claim-adjudication veto included; failed legal-stop gates are recorded in stop_decision.blockedBy as synthesis evidence.
          1a. After 3 consecutive failed iterations (status error or timeout), halt the loop and enter synthesis with stop reason error and partial findings.
          2. if stuck_count >= config.stuckThreshold: STUCK_RECOVERY
          3. if convergence_mode == "off": skip convergence stop candidates and continue.
          3a. if stop_policy == "max-iterations" AND iteration_count < max_iterations: the vote is telemetry only and the decision is CONTINUE.
          4. a STOP candidate exists when every dimension is clean (all_dimensions_clean) or the weighted vote passes (composite_converged). The vote cutoffs follow the configured convergenceThreshold.
          4a. if iteration_count < min_iterations: CONTINUE (minIterationsNotReached). min_iterations is config.minIterations, else antiConvergence.minIterations, capped at max_iterations; like research, the floor runs on a candidate before the legal-stop gates.
          5. the nine legal-stop gates run on a candidate: convergenceGate, dimensionCoverageGate, p0ResolutionGate, evidenceDensityGate, hotspotSaturationGate, claimAdjudicationGate, fixCompletenessReplayGate, candidateCoverageGate, graphlessFallbackGate. Any failed gate gives BLOCKED (blockedStop).
          6. the graph decision settles the candidate: STOP_ALLOWED keeps STOP, STOP_BLOCKED gives BLOCKED, CONTINUE or a missing graph decision gives CONTINUE.
          Bind the outputs from stop_decision, where G is stop_decision.gates and I is stop_decision.inputs.review:
            decision = stop_decision.decision
            stopReason = stop_decision.stopReason
            reason = stop_decision.stopReason when it is not null, else stop_decision.reasonDetail
            reason_detail = stop_decision.reasonDetail
            weighted_stop_score = stop_decision.weightedStopScore
            stop_blocked = (stop_decision.decision == "BLOCKED")
            blocked_by_json = stop_decision.blockedBy
            blocked_by_csv = the members of stop_decision.blockedBy joined with commas
            convergence_gate_pass = G.convergenceGate.pass
            convergence_gate_score = G.convergenceGate.score
            dimension_coverage_gate_pass = G.dimensionCoverageGate.pass
            dimension_coverage_gate_missing_json = G.dimensionCoverageGate.missing
            dimension_coverage_gate_covered_json = the configured review dimensions that are not in G.dimensionCoverageGate.missing
            p0_resolution_gate_pass = G.p0ResolutionGate.pass
            active_p0_count = G.p0ResolutionGate.activeP0
            evidence_density_gate_pass = G.evidenceDensityGate.pass
            avg_evidence_per_finding = I.evidenceBackedP0P1, the count of active P0 and P1 findings that carry file:line evidence
            hotspot_saturation_gate_pass = G.hotspotSaturationGate.pass
            claim_adjudication_gate_pass = G.claimAdjudicationGate.pass
            claim_adjudication_active_count = I.activeP0 plus I.activeP1
            fix_completeness_replay_gate_pass = G.fixCompletenessReplayGate.pass
            security_sensitive_fix_scope = I.securitySensitiveFixScope
            fix_completeness_replay_required_rows = I.fixReplayRequiredRows
            fix_completeness_replay_passing_rows = I.fixReplayPassingRows
            candidate_coverage_gate_pass = G.candidateCoverageGate.pass
            candidate_coverage_search_debt_json = I.searchDebt
            candidate_coverage_missing_json = the I.requiredBugClasses entries that are not in I.candidateCovered
            graphless_fallback_gate_pass = G.graphlessFallbackGate.pass
            graphless_fallback_mode = I.graphCoverageMode
            graphless_fallback_missing_json = the I.requiredBugClasses entries that are not in I.fallbackCoveredBugClasses
            graphless_fallback_unavailability_reason = the explicit reason when I.graphCoverageMode == "unavailable_blocked", else empty string
            graph_blocker_detail_json = graph_blockers_json
            recovery_strategy = one line naming the next review action for the gates in stop_decision.blockedBy. When candidateCoverageGate and graphlessFallbackGate both fail, combine their guidance. When I.unfinishedCoreProtocols is non-empty, name them and say the last iteration under the cap is reserved for them, each needing file:line evidence.
          If stop_decision is missing from the step_graph_convergence output, do not decide by hand: halt and report the script output.

              Options:
              A) Retry: dispatch the next iteration with the same dimension
              B) Skip: dispatch the next iteration with the next dimension
              C) Cancel: end the run with no synthesis
            wait_for: "User response"
            after_gate_refresh_lock: gate_defaults.after_gate_refresh_lock
            on_A: { set: { decision: "CONTINUE" }, proceed_to: step_normalize_pause_events }
            on_B: { action: "Advance next_dimension to the next uncovered dimension in strategy.md", set: { decision: "CONTINUE" }, proceed_to: step_normalize_pause_events }
            on_C: { action: "Cancel workflow", status: "CANCELLED", release_lock: step_release_lock }
          note: "The failure count is rebuilt from the state log, so this gate asks again after each further failed iteration and stops asking once an iteration completes."
        if_stop:
          record_terminal_gates:

            note: "The script computes all three gates from the config, state log and registry: evidence by the evidenceDensityGate rule, coverage by the dimensionCoverageGate rule, and scope by matching every open finding's file against reviewScopeFiles.             on_error: "When the script exits non-zero or prints status error, fail closed: bind terminal_gate_results_json to three rows, evidence, scope and coverage, each with status fail, reasonCode gate-script-error and evidenceDigest the sha256 of the script's error text, and bind failed_required_gate_ids to [\"evidence\",\"scope\",\"coverage\"]."
            evaluate: |
              terminal_stop_reason = reason when it is maxIterationsReached, maxDurationReached, manualStop or error, else "converged". Read reason, never stopReason: every manual stop sets reason to manualStop while stopReason may still hold blockedStop or stuckRecovery
            outputs:
              - terminal_gate_results_json: "Tool terminal_gate_results_json: three rows {gateId, status, reasonCode, evidenceDigest} in the order evidence, scope, coverage"
              - failed_required_gate_ids: "Tool failed_required_gate_ids: the gateIds whose status is fail, in that order; [] when all pass"
              - terminal_stop_reason: "From evaluate"
            append_jsonl: '{"stem":"deep_review.terminal_gates_recorded","scope":{"runId":"{session_id}","sessionId":"{session_id}","generation":{generation},"iterationId":"iteration-{current_iteration}"},"data":{"stopReason":"{terminal_stop_reason}","gateResults":{terminal_gate_results_json},"failedRequiredGateIds":{failed_required_gate_ids},"appendPosition":{current_iteration}}}'
            log: "Terminal gates recorded at stop {terminal_stop_reason}. Failed required gates: {failed_required_gate_ids}"
          branch_on: "convergence_mode == 'divergent' AND stop_decision.reasonDetail == 'all_dimensions_clean'"
          if_divergent_eligible:
            eligibility_contract:
              required_decision: "STOP"
              eligible_reasons: [all_dimensions_clean]
              excluded_reasons: [maxIterationsReached, blockedStop, stuckRecovery, error, manualStop, userPaused, maxDurationReached]
              fail_closed: "Any missing or ambiguous reason uses if_existing_terminal_path without preparing a pivot. The nine legal-stop gates have already run and remain unchanged."
              frontier_gate: "Under depth_mode == 'expansive' the pivot is eligible only once registry.frontier holds no open or active node. A candidate the pivot accepts re-enters breadth through step_frontier_admit as a frontier_proposal row, and the pivot may seed from nodes closed exhausted or blocked."

step_check_convergence:
        action: "Branch on the stop decision that convergence.cjs computed in step_graph_convergence"
        algorithm: |
          The whole stop decision is computed by convergence.cjs from the loop config, the state log, the findings registry and the coverage graph. This step performs no arithmetic and never recomputes or overrides the result.
          The ratio signals leave out every iteration whose status is thought, error or timeout, and stuck_count is loop_counters_json.stuckCount.
          The script applies this fixed order, first match wins:
          1. if iteration_count >= max_iterations: STOP (maxIterationsReached). This terminal cap bypasses every guard and veto; failed guards are recorded in stop_decision.blockedBy as synthesis evidence.
          1a. After 3 consecutive failed iterations (status error or timeout), halt the loop and enter synthesis with stop reason error and partial findings.
          2. if stuck_count >= config.stuckThreshold: STUCK_RECOVERY
          3. if convergence_mode == "off": skip convergence stop candidates and continue.
          3a. if stop_policy == "max-iterations" AND iteration_count < max_iterations: the vote is telemetry only and the decision is CONTINUE.
          4. a STOP candidate exists when every question is answered (all_questions_answered) or the weighted vote passes (composite_converged).
          5. if iteration_count < effective_min_iterations: CONTINUE (minIterationsNotReached)
          6. a failed quality guard turns the candidate into CONTINUE (qualityGuardFailed)
          7. the graph decision settles the candidate: STOP_ALLOWED keeps STOP, STOP_BLOCKED gives BLOCKED, CONTINUE or a missing graph decision gives CONTINUE.
          Bind the outputs from stop_decision:

  step_frontier_seed:
    description: "Seed the depth-1 frontier before any fan-out lineage spawns"
    enabled_when: "depth_mode == 'expansive'"
    skip_when: "config.fanout is absent"
    dry_run_boundary: "state-mutation"
    instructions: |
      Fan-out jumps from step_fanout_spawn to phase_synthesis and never reaches
      phase_init, so the seed runs here or not at all. fanout-run.cjs then splits
      the seeded depth-1 nodes round-robin across the lineages.
      Decompose {research_topic} into 3 to 6 depth-1 sub-questions that together
      cover the topic. Write them as a JSON array to {artifact_dir}/frontier-seed.json,
      one object per sub-question: {"question": "...", "scope": "<path, module or
      area>", "evidence": "<repo path or topic phrase that grounds it>"}. Then run
      the command. frontier-admit.cjs is the only writer of the node store and the
      frontier inbox rows; never write either file by hand.
    command: 'node .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed --artifact-dir "{artifact_dir}" --iteration 0 --input "{artifact_dir}/frontier-seed.json"'
    working_directory: "{repo_root}"
    outputs:
      - frontier_seed_json: "The JSON line the command prints: admitted node ids and pruned {nodeId, reason} rows"
    on_failure: "Halt with the command's stderr line. A run that asked for expansive depth must not start without its seeded frontier."

151:| **MAD noise floor** | 0.25 | The latest ratio measured against statistical noise |
156:Any new P0 forces another iteration. The override sets `newFindingsRatio` to at least 0.50, so a fresh blocker cannot be averaged away. The shipped config adds `antiConvergence.minIterations` of 2 and a `stopPolicy` of `fail-closed`, and the runtime capability loader rejects a missing or permissive stop policy. The floor holds a review run at `CONTINUE` with reason `minIterationsNotReached` until its iteration count reaches `minIterations`, even when every dimension is clean.
158:`--depth-mode=expansive` is the one stop-path change that grows work instead of ending it. A seed step writes the depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, the active node's next uncovered dimension steers the iteration, and an admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, pruning the proposal on the first failure. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, which no default run creates, and an open node is filed as open search debt, so the run spends the iteration that node still needs. The frontier veto never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the floor above, and stuck recovery closes the active node as `exhausted`. The caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.

---
title: "Depth mode flag (--depth-mode)"
description: "The --depth-mode flag on /deep:research and /deep:review selects default or expansive depth, and leaves the registry-driven workflowMode, runtimeLoopType and backendKind classification unchanged."
trigger_phrases:
  - "--depth-mode"
  - "depth mode flag"
  - "expansive depth flag"
  - "deep loop depth mode"
version: 1.0.1.0
---

# Depth mode flag (--depth-mode)

## 1. OVERVIEW

`--depth-mode` is a command-level option on `/deep:research` and `/deep:review`. It takes `default` or `expansive`. The flag is not part of `mode-registry.json`, so the hub classifies a request into the same workflow mode with or without it. The behavior the flag turns on lives in the research and review packets.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Hub Classification Unchanged

`mode-registry.json` carries the `workflowMode`, `runtimeLoopType` and `backendKind` discriminator for `research` and `review`, and it has no depth field. Routing resolves the same packet whether the flag is present or not.

### Flag Routing

The command workflow reads `--depth-mode` and binds it as `depth_mode`. Only the value `expansive` reaches the runtime. `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` adds `depthMode` to its stop-decision config only when the value is `expansive`, and `runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` passes `depthMode` to fan-out lineages only when the value is `expansive`. A default run carries no depth key in either place.

### Packet Behavior

The expansive behavior is documented in the packet catalogs: [expansive depth mode for research](../../deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md) and [expansive depth mode for review](../../deep-review/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md).

---

## 3. SOURCE FILES

### Implementation

| File | Layer | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/mode-registry.json` | Registry | Holds the discriminator for `research` and `review`, with no depth field. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/research.md` | Command doc | The `--depth-mode` flag row for research. |
| `.skilled/commands/deep/review.md` | Command doc | The `--depth-mode` flag row for review. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` | Script | Adds `depthMode` to the stop-decision config only when expansive. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` | Script | Passes `depthMode` to lineages only when expansive. |

### Validation And Tests

| File | Type | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md` | Manual playbook | DR-068: the flag defaults to `default` and a default run matches the baseline. |
| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md` | Manual playbook | DRV-076: the review flag defaults to `default` and a default run matches the baseline. |

 # deep-research
@@ -124,6 +124,8 @@ The shipped config floors convergence at 3 rounds, sets `stuckThreshold` to 3 an
 
 `--convergence-mode` changes the stop path. `off` disables convergence STOP while the hard caps stay active. `sliding-window` scores novelty over a bounded window. `divergent` sends an eligible legal stop into one bounded pivot round instead of synthesis, then returns to the loop.
 
+`--depth-mode=expansive` lets a run grow its own questions. A seed step writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1, and the leaf may propose children only when its prompt carries a frontier block. An admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, and prunes the proposal on the first failure. Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first and writes it as a frontier line, so visiting a node does not read as answering it. While a node stays open the frontier vetoes a soft convergence stop, which never outranks the iteration cap or the other hard stops, and the caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`.
+

function frontierNodeVisits(nodeId, iterationRecords) {
  const escaped = String(nodeId).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
  const marker = new RegExp(`(?:^|[^\\w-])Frontier node ${escaped}(?![\\w-])`);
  return (Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords : [])
    .filter((record) => typeof record?.focus === 'string' && marker.test(record.focus))
    .length;
}

function pickFrontierFocus(frontier, iterationRecords) {
  const open = (Array.isArray(frontier?.nodes) ? frontier.nodes : [])
    .filter((node) => node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active');
  if (open.length === 0) return '';
  // Breadth first: the shallowest node, then one not yet visited, then the oldest.
  const ranked = open
    .map((node) => ({ node, visits: frontierNodeVisits(node.nodeId, iterationRecords) }))
    .sort((left, right) => (left.node.depth - right.node.depth)
      || (left.visits - right.visits)
      || ((left.node.iteration ?? 0) - (right.node.iteration ?? 0))
      || left.node.nodeId.localeCompare(right.node.nodeId));
  const chosen = ranked[0].node;
  const scope = Array.isArray(chosen.scope) ? chosen.scope.join(', ') : (chosen.scope || 'unscoped');
  return `Frontier node ${chosen.nodeId} (depth ${chosen.depth}, scope ${scope}): ${chosen.question}`;
}

// Depth bands share maxIterations 50/35/15 by default (depths 0-1, 2, 3). A band's
// unspent share rolls down to the next band only once the band has no open node,
// and never up. When a band's spend reaches its share, its open nodes close as
// exhausted with reason budget, so the run stops waiting on work it cannot fund.
// An open node whose question an iteration already answered is marked answered
// first: the budget closes only work still outstanding, and an answered node
// never holds its band open against the roll-down.
function applyFrontierBudget(nodes, iterationRecords, maxIterations, budgetSplit, answeredNodeIds = new Set()) {
  const split = budgetSplit && typeof budgetSplit === 'object' ? budgetSplit : {};
  const shares = [
    Number.isFinite(split.depth1) ? split.depth1 : 0.5,
    Number.isFinite(split.depth2) ? split.depth2 : 0.35,
    Number.isFinite(split.depth3) ? split.depth3 : 0.15,
  ];
  const cap = Number.isFinite(maxIterations) && maxIterations > 0 ? maxIterations : 10;
  const bandOf = (depth) => (depth <= 1 ? 0 : depth === 2 ? 1 : 2);
  const spent = [0, 0, 0];
  for (const node of nodes) {
    const visits = frontierNodeVisits(node.nodeId, (Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords : [])
      .filter((record) => !['error', 'failed', 'failure', 'timeout'].includes(String(record?.status || '').toLowerCase())));
    spent[bandOf(node.depth)] += visits;
  }
  let result = nodes.map((node) => ((node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active') && answeredNodeIds.has(node.nodeId)
    ? { ...node, status: 'answered', reason: null }
    : { ...node }));
  const budget = {};
  let carry = 0;
  for (let band = 0; band < 3; band += 1) {
    const allotted = cap * shares[band] + carry;
    if (spent[band] >= allotted) {
      result = result.map((node) => (bandOf(node.depth) === band && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active')
        ? { ...node, status: 'exhausted', reason: 'budget' }
        : node));
    }
    const bandOpen = result.some((node) => bandOf(node.depth) === band && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active'));
    carry = bandOpen ? 0 : Math.max(0, allotted - spent[band]);
    budget[`depth${band + 1}`] = { allotted: Math.round(allotted * 100) / 100, spent: spent[band] };
  }
  return { nodes: result, budget };
}

function resolveNextFocus(registry, iterationFiles, iterationRecords) {
  const rejectedIndex = registry.rejectedPatternIndex;
  const latestBlockedStop = Array.isArray(registry.blockedStopHistory)
    ? registry.blockedStopHistory.at(-1)
    : null;
  const latestIteration = Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords.at(-1) : null;

  if (registry.divergence?.currentFocus
    && isFiniteNumber(registry.divergence.activeSourceIteration)
    && registry.divergence.activeSourceIteration > (readIterationNumber(latestIteration) ?? 0)) {
    return registry.divergence.currentFocus;
  }

  if (latestBlockedStop) {
    const latestBlockedTimestamp = getTimestampValue(latestBlockedStop.timestamp);
    const latestIterationTimestamp = getTimestampValue(latestIteration?.timestamp);

    if (latestBlockedTimestamp > latestIterationTimestamp) {
      const recoveryStrategy = latestBlockedStop.recoveryStrategy || '[No recovery strategy recorded]';
      const recoveryMatch = findRejectedPatternMatch(
        { text: recoveryStrategy, category: 'recovery' },
        rejectedIndex,
        { category: 'recovery' },
      );
      if (!recoveryMatch) {
        return [
          `BLOCKED on: ${formatBlockedByList(latestBlockedStop.blockedBy)}`,
          `Recovery: ${recoveryStrategy}`,
          'Address the blocking gates before the next iteration.',
        ].join('\n');
      }

| `novelty below threshold` | `converged` |
| `all_questions_answered` | `converged` |
| `max_iterations_reached` | `maxIterationsReached` |
| `paused` | `userPaused` |
| `stuck_detected` | `stuckRecovery` |
| `stuck_unrecoverable` | `stuckRecovery` |

---

## 3. LIVE ALGORITHM

### Decision Order

```text
1. If iteration count >= maxIterations, STOP with maxIterationsReached.
2. After 3 consecutive failed iterations, STOP with stop reason error.
3. If stuckCount >= stuckThreshold, enter stuck recovery.
4. If convergenceMode is off or stopPolicy is max-iterations, CONTINUE.
5. If all key questions have evidence-backed answers, nominate STOP. Otherwise compute composite convergence signals and nominate STOP when the weighted stop score > 0.60. With no candidate, CONTINUE.
6. If iteration count < minIterations, CONTINUE with minIterationsNotReached.
7. If depthMode is expansive and a frontier node is open or active, CONTINUE with frontierOpen.
8. Evaluate legal-stop gates for every STOP candidate except hard max-iteration stop.
9. Block every non-terminal STOP until a graph decision exists.
10. If gates pass, pivot for an eligible divergent reason or STOP normally; otherwise emit blockedStop and CONTINUE with recovery focus.
```

### Composite Signal Weights

| Signal | Weight | Min Iterations | Measures |
===
- When the graph convergence verdict is `STOP_BLOCKED`, fold the graph blocker gate name into `blockedBy` and preserve the structured blocker objects under `graphBlockerDetail`.

### Decision Priority

Checks are evaluated in this order (first match wins), as `buildStopDecision` in `runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs` runs them:

1. **Max iterations** -- hard cap, always checked first. It outranks every veto below.
2. **Consecutive failures** -- three trailing failed iterations stop the run with stop reason `error`.
3. **Stuck detection** -- `stuckThreshold` (2) consecutive no-progress iterations route to stuck recovery.
4. **Convergence mode off** -- `convergenceMode: off` returns `CONTINUE` (`convergenceModeOff`).
5. **Stop policy max-iterations** -- `stopPolicy: max-iterations` returns `CONTINUE` (`stopPolicyMaxIterations`).
6. **Stop candidate** -- all dimensions covered and clean (every configured dimension covered, the four by default, no active P0/P1, coverage stabilized) is a hard candidate; otherwise the 3-signal composite vote with threshold 0.60 is a soft one. No candidate returns `CONTINUE` (`noStopCandidate`).
7. **Iteration floor** -- below `minIterations` the candidate returns `CONTINUE` (`minIterationsNotReached`).
8. **Frontier veto** -- under `--depth-mode=expansive`, an open frontier node turns the candidate into `CONTINUE` (`frontierOpen`).
9. **Legal-stop gate bundle** -- review-specific stop gates. If the candidate fails any gate, persist `stopReason=blockedStop` and continue.
10. **Graph decision** -- the candidate is combined with the graph convergence verdict: `STOP_ALLOWED` stops, `STOP_BLOCKED` blocks the stop and `CONTINUE` continues the run.

---

## 2. HARD STOPS

Hard stops are evaluated first and override all other signals.

Restart archives the old research tree, mints a fresh session identifier, increments generation, and appends a typed `restarted` event. `completed-continue` is live through `--extend=N` on a completed run: it keeps the session identifier and generation, raises `maxIterations` by N, appends a typed `run_extended` event, and copies the prior synthesis to a versioned snapshot before the first new iteration.
